# Thailand payroll obligations — independent inventory

Scope: employment and payroll periods from **1 December 2025**, all provinces, relevant districts, sectors, occupations, worker classes and insurance memberships. This is the Thailand denominator for certification, not a claim of certification. Every ID remains open until its enacted instrument and amendment history, required inputs, production output, boundary probes and saved/reversal path have been checked. Source review date: 27 September 2026; source and coverage review 28 September 2026 (see below).

| Denominator decision                        | Included or excluded scope                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | Source/closure condition                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| ------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Included: employer payroll                  | Every Thai-covered employee and employing entity; ordinary and special workers, foreign workers, all province/district/sector/skill wages, cash and noncash pay, tax, contributions, deductions, leave, working time and exit.               | No category is removed on a UI label alone. Map the Labour Protection Act, Revenue Code, SSO/WCF and each special regulation to actual worker/employer facts and dated payment.                                                                                                                                |
| Included: employer HR and workplace duties  | Contract, work rules, age and pregnancy protection, collective terms, training fund, safety, foreign-work authorisation, records, notifications and termination, including duties whose output is a filing, training record or human action. | The product must classify and calculate the duties it handles, retain any evidence it uses and warn or refuse when required facts are absent. Filing, training and other employer acts performed outside the product remain legal-boundary notes; this inventory does not require the product to execute them. |
| Included: insurer/claim interfaces          | SSO and WCF benefits/claims when payroll or HR must classify absence, retain insured history, present a claim or avoid an improper wage offset.                                                                                              | Classify insurer payer versus employer payer expressly. A benefit the insurer pays is not an employer gross-pay line merely because it appears on a payslip.                                                                                                                                                   |
| Conditional or excluded only by enacted law | Government, state enterprise, nonbusiness household, agriculture, seafaring, fishing, home work, genuine trainees and service payees may follow different or no particular **instrument**.                                                   | Exclusion is branch-specific, never a whole-person exemption. TH-WAGE-05, TH-HR-01 and TH-HR-17–21 remain open pending signed-instrument reconciliation and case probes.                                                                                                                                       |
| Outside this register                       | Corporate income tax, VAT, immigration visas unrelated to employment authorisation, general commercial invoicing and insurer-only medical adjudication with no employer/HR interface.                                                        | These do not satisfy or reduce any included payroll/HR obligation. If the product undertakes such a service later, add its own source and branch rather than silently expanding this register.                                                                                                                 |

The row count below is a **known-obligation inventory**, not a proven exhaustive legal denominator. Full instrument-level coverage still requires issuer-index and Gazette amendment reconciliation, including subsidiary rules for each sector and worker class. Until then no Thailand percentage or certification can be asserted.

An [18 August 2026 Ministry announcement](https://minister.mol.go.th/news/%E0%B8%88%E0%B8%B8%E0%B8%A5%E0%B8%9E%E0%B8%B1%E0%B8%99%E0%B8%98%E0%B9%8C-%E0%B9%80%E0%B8%9C%E0%B8%A2-%E0%B8%84%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%A1-%E0%B9%84%E0%B8%9F%E0%B9%80%E0%B8%82%E0%B8%B5%E0%B8%A2%E0%B8%A7%E0%B8%81%E0%B8%8E%E0%B8%AB%E0%B8%A1%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%A2%E0%B9%81%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%99-4-%E0%B8%89%E0%B8%9A%E0%B8%B1%E0%B8%9A-%E0%B8%A2%E0%B8%81%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%B0%E0%B8%94%E0%B8%B1%E0%B8%9A%E0%B8%AA%E0%B8%A7%E0%B8%B1%E0%B8%AA%E0%B8%94%E0%B8%B4%E0%B8%81%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%A3-%E0%B8%84%E0%B8%99%E0%B8%9B%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%B0%E0%B8%88%E0%B8%B3%E0%B9%80%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%B7%E0%B8%AD-%E0%B8%9C%E0%B8%B9%E0%B9%89%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%B1%E0%B8%9A%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%99%E0%B9%84%E0%B8%9B%E0%B8%97%E0%B8%B3%E0%B8%97%E0%B8%B5%E0%B9%88%E0%B8%9A%E0%B9%89%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%99-%E0%B8%A5%E0%B8%B9%E0%B8%81%E0%B8%88%E0%B9%89%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%B1%E0%B8%90%E0%B8%A7%E0%B8%B4%E0%B8%AA%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%AB%E0%B8%81%E0%B8%B4%E0%B8%88%E0%B8%81%E0%B8%A5%E0%B8%B8%E0%B9%88%E0%B8%A1%E0%B8%AA%E0%B8%A1%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%AA%E0%B9%80%E0%B8%97%E0%B9%88%E0%B8%B2%E0%B9%80%E0%B8%97%E0%B8%B5%E0%B8%A2%E0%B8%A1) describes Cabinet-approved **draft** measures for seafarers, home workers and state-enterprise spouses. It supplies no operative replacement date or rate; TH-HR-19–21 retain their enacted-law branches pending Gazette publication.

## Source hierarchy and dated cutovers

Use the signed Thai enactment or Gazette notice first, then the issuing authority's table or guidance. A news story, translation, brochure or payslip can corroborate but cannot silently amend a signed instrument. The [Ministry of Labour wage index](https://www.mol.go.th/%E0%B8%AD%E0%B8%B1%E0%B8%95%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%84%E0%B9%88%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%88%E0%B9%89%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%82%E0%B8%B1%E0%B9%89%E0%B8%99%E0%B8%95%E0%B9%88%E0%B8%B3) still lists geographic Minimum Wage Notice 14 separately from skill-standard Notices 13–15. The Revenue Department's [Order P.96/2543](https://www.rd.go.th/3558.html) explicitly distinguishes normal pay, irregular pay, December reconciliation, employer-paid tax and a midyear hire's remaining pay occasions.

### Notice 14 geographic rate denominator

The Ministry's [signed Notice 14, explanation and printed provincial table](https://www.mol.go.th/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2025/07/%E0%B8%9B%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%B0%E0%B8%81%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%A8-%E0%B8%84%E0%B8%88.%E0%B8%82%E0%B8%B1%E0%B9%89%E0%B8%99%E0%B8%95%E0%B9%88%E0%B8%B3-%E0%B8%8914-%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%A7%E0%B8%A1.pdf), dated 17 June and effective **1 July 2025**, assign one base daily rate to each of 77 provinces. The table below was reconciled against the Ministry's printed table: 77 distinct province names in 16 base-rate groups. The three district and two sector overrides are additional selectors, not extra provinces. Official transliterations vary (`Ko`/`Koh`, `Phang Nga`/`Phangna`), so inputs need canonical province/district identifiers rather than uncontrolled text.

| THB/day | Base province assignment                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ------: | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
|     400 | Bangkok; Chachoengsao; Chon Buri; Phuket; Rayong.                                                                                                                                          |
|     372 | Nakhon Pathom; Nonthaburi; Pathum Thani; Samut Prakan; Samut Sakhon.                                                                                                                       |
|     359 | Nakhon Ratchasima.                                                                                                                                                                         |
|     358 | Samut Songkhram.                                                                                                                                                                           |
|     357 | Khon Kaen; Chiang Mai; Prachin Buri; Phra Nakhon Si Ayutthaya; Saraburi.                                                                                                                   |
|     356 | Lop Buri.                                                                                                                                                                                  |
|     355 | Nakhon Nayok; Suphan Buri; Nong Khai.                                                                                                                                                      |
|     354 | Krabi; Trat.                                                                                                                                                                               |
|     352 | Kanchanaburi; Chanthaburi; Chiang Rai; Tak; Nakhon Phanom; Buriram; Prachuap Khiri Khan; Phang Nga; Phitsanulok; Mukdahan; Sakon Nakhon; Songkhla; Sa Kaeo; Surat Thani; Ubon Ratchathani. |
|     351 | Chumphon; Phetchaburi; Surin.                                                                                                                                                              |
|     350 | Nakhon Sawan; Yasothon; Lamphun.                                                                                                                                                           |
|     349 | Kalasin; Nakhon Si Thammarat; Bueng Kan; Phetchabun; Roi Et.                                                                                                                               |
|     348 | Chai Nat; Chaiyaphum; Phatthalung; Sing Buri; Ang Thong.                                                                                                                                   |
|     347 | Kamphaeng Phet; Phichit; Maha Sarakham; Mae Hong Son; Ranong; Ratchaburi; Lampang; Loei; Sisaket; Satun; Sukhothai; Nong Bua Lamphu; Amnat Charoen; Udon Thani; Uttaradit; Uthai Thani.    |
|     345 | Trang; Nan; Phayao; Phrae.                                                                                                                                                                 |
|     337 | Narathiwat; Pattani; Yala.                                                                                                                                                                 |

The Ministry's [English translation of Notice 14](https://www.mol.go.th/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2025/08/%E0%B9%81%E0%B8%9B%E0%B8%A5%E0%B8%9B%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%B0%E0%B8%81%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%A8-%E0%B8%89%E0%B8%9A%E0%B8%B1%E0%B8%9A%E0%B8%97%E0%B8%B5%E0%B9%88-14-_1_-1.pdf) is labelled an unofficial translation; use it only to corroborate English province/district spellings (it spells the same province both Phangna and Phang Nga). District overrides: **Ko Samui, Surat Thani THB400**; **Mueang Chiang Mai, Chiang Mai THB380**; **Hat Yai, Songkhla THB380**. Nationwide sector overrides: **hotel types 2–4 THB400** and **Service Establishment Act businesses THB400**. The hotel's legal type is determined by the 2023 hotel-type regulation, not by its name, star rating or generic hospitality industry: type 1 supplies only rooms and has at most 50; type 2 supplies only rooms with more than 50, or rooms plus food facilities; type 3 adds either a legally classified service establishment or conference room; type 4 has both. A restaurant, bar or massage label alone does not establish Service Establishment Act status. The Ministry's [Notice 14 explanation, items 7 and 12–13](https://www.mol.go.th/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2025/07/%E0%B8%9B%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%B0%E0%B8%81%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%A8-%E0%B8%84%E0%B8%88.%E0%B8%82%E0%B8%B1%E0%B9%89%E0%B8%99%E0%B8%95%E0%B9%88%E0%B8%B3-%E0%B8%8914-%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%A7%E0%B8%A1.pdf) states these distinctions. Notice 14 cl.19 fixes the daily comparator at the normal day (up to seven hours for specified hazardous work, otherwise up to eight) even if fewer hours are worked.

[Labour Protection Act ss.87–90](https://searchlaw.ocs.go.th/council-of-state/#/public/doc/ZGN3NXk0eENvNjBSdjRnT2NsdjFTQT09) (Council of State consolidation through Act No.9 B.E.2568) permit location/industry/occupation-specific rates, but the Wage Committee must issue the rate for Gazette publication before it binds employers. A 2026 provincial subcommittee review or proposal is therefore not itself a new local wage floor. The operative locality/sector selectors presently inventoried are Notice 14's province, three district and two sector branches; any later Gazette notice still requires index reconciliation. Section 90 separately requires display of the effective notice at the workplace (TH-WAGE-06).

### s.33 contribution parameters at the December/January boundary

The [2026 wage-base regulation](https://ratchakitcha.soc.go.th/documents/98728.pdf) changes the cap, not the ordinary 5% contribution percentage. That 5% is set by the [Ministerial Regulation on Social Security Fund contribution rates B.E.2565](https://infocenter.oic.go.th/FILEWEB/CABINFOCENTER2/DRAWER056/GENERAL/DATA0000/00000773.PDF) (Gazette vol.139 part 60 Kor, 30 September 2022; signed 27 September 2022), cl.3(2) and Schedule B from 1 January 2023: employer and insured each 1.5% (injury, sickness, invalidity, death, maternity) + 3% (child support, old age) + 0.5% (unemployment) = 5%; the government pays 1.5% + 1% + 0.25%. The [SSO ministerial-regulation index](https://infocenter.oic.go.th/%E0%B8%AA%E0%B8%B3%E0%B8%99%E0%B8%B1%E0%B8%81%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%99%E0%B8%9B%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%B0%E0%B8%81%E0%B8%B1%E0%B8%99%E0%B8%AA%E0%B8%B1%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%84%E0%B8%A1/topic.php?gid=8151&mid=1017) lists no later ordinary-rate regulation; the flood notice below is a separate temporary override. The [flood-relief notice](https://ratchakitcha.soc.go.th/documents/100888.pdf) changes both employee and employer percentages to 3% only for registered employers and s.33 insured workers in **Trang, Nakhon Si Thammarat, Narathiwat, Pattani, Phatthalung, Yala, Songkhla, Satun or Surat Thani** for wage months December 2025–May 2026. It was published on 8 January 2026, so December adjustments and refunds need preserved prior assessments. Rounding is statutory: [Social Security Act s.46](https://searchlaw.ocs.go.th/council-of-state/#/public/doc/alJWY29wVXFRUUo0WkF2MTEwSndpQT09), last paragraph (text substituted by Act No.3 B.E.2542), counts each person's contribution fraction of 50 satang or more as one baht and drops a smaller fraction, and computes an insured person's contributions separately on the wages from each employer (TH-SS-11). Example: THB10,250 at 3% = 307.50 → THB308.

| Wage month and class                          |       Base floor/cap | Employee and employer each, at the cap | Next boundary                 |
| --------------------------------------------- | -------------------: | -------------------------------------: | ----------------------------- |
| December 2025, ordinary s.33                  | THB1,650 / THB15,000 |                           THB750 at 5% | January cap change            |
| December 2025, covered flood locality s.33    | THB1,650 / THB15,000 |                           THB450 at 3% | January cap change            |
| January–May 2026, ordinary s.33               | THB1,650 / THB17,500 |                           THB875 at 5% | May/June flood expiry         |
| January–May 2026, covered flood locality s.33 | THB1,650 / THB17,500 |                           THB525 at 3% | June returns to ordinary rate |

The notice separately states **THB283 per month** for eligible registered s.39 insured members; that is not an employer percentage of salary. The registration locality and class must be recorded rather than inferred solely from a worksite address.

## Obligation register

`Open` means no end-to-end production proof. `External` means a payroll result alone cannot fulfill filing, remittance, notification or insurer-claim evidence; it still belongs in the denominator. IDs are stable for tests and trace records.

Several sites in one week: `work_days.worksite` records the site a day was worked and overrides the terms' worksite for that day (cl.20; 29 Sep 2026); golden 'one person working several sites in the same week is held to each work day’s own site' (daily 370 on Mae Rim terms, Mon 2 Feb 2026 recorded at Mueang Chiang Mai blocks on that 1 day at 380, Tue 3 Feb recorded at Mae Rim passes; 380 meets both; a bare 'Chiang Mai' work-day site refuses). Still open: 30 June/1 July 2025 cutover (before the first version). The income is ordinary s.40(1) income on the employee's ภ.ง.ด.91 (2025 instructions pp.1–2 list the exit payment, statutory severance included, with under five years' service among salary and wages, taxed together with other income), but no Revenue order read prescribes its withholding. Owner rule 2026-09-28: law silent on the method; default: Revenue Code s.50(1) para.1 read literally for a payment made once (one payment, "as if paid for the whole year", i.e. added to the year's regular pay), priced by the P.96 cl.1(5) mechanics the PIT line already uses — the annual tax with the taxable remainder (SEVERANCE_PAY + NOTICE_IN_LIEU less the MR No.126 cl.2(51) exemption) less the annual tax without it, withheld whole in the exit month on the PIT line (PIT `assessed_on` adds the remainder only where service is under five years; SEVERANCE_TAX's refusal rule removed; every version, unchanged law fixed in place); lawful because it is s.50(1)'s own computation and equals the increment the payment adds to the employee's ภ.ง.ด.91 liability. Decision reason closed. Goldens 'severance is withheld…' TAX-RETIRE-3Y (180,000 on retirement, 30,000 a month → PIT 12,639.58 on 210,000; SEVERANCE_TAX nil) and 's.17/1 pay in lieu as a catalogue line' NIL-3Y (30,000 taxable notice pay → PIT 1,664.58 on 60,000). Filing: the ภ.ง.ด.1 line split for this income stays External. Also noted: DG No.45 cl.3(2)–(3) cap a cl.1(ง) payment's s.48(5) deduction at last month's salary × years; the notice-pay probe (150,000 ≤ 150,000 × 20) does not reach the cap and the cap is not probed. Explicit NOT_REGISTERED now assesses the exit-payment withholding under Revenue Code s.50(1); this seed change awaits the final gate. |

### Employment, working conditions and non-payroll compliance branches

The Ministry's [Labour Protection Act text](https://www.mol.go.th/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/1998/01/Labour_Protection_Act_BE2541.pdf) is an annotated English consolidation through earlier amendments, **not** proof that each provision is current in 2025–26. Its operative Thai amendments, ministerial regulations and sector exceptions must be checked before sealing these rows. [Labour Protection Act No. 9](https://ratchakitcha.soc.go.th/documents/89818.pdf) is a later signed amendment already separated above. The [Ministry's current work-rules explanation](https://lb.mol.go.th/en/preparation-of-work-regulations) explicitly says amended s.108 no longer requires sending the rules to the Director-General, despite that instruction in the older English PDF.

These rows are explicit **open** obligations, not evidence that every possible subsidiary regulation has been found. Their primary-source inventory and applicability must be reconciled by sector, worker type and date before the denominator can be called exhaustive.

### 26 September 2026 instrument-index check

- **Minimum wages.** The [Ministry geographic-wage index](https://www.mol.go.th/%E0%B8%AD%E0%B8%B1%E0%B8%95%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%84%E0%B9%88%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%88%E0%B9%89%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%82%E0%B8%B1%E0%B9%89%E0%B8%99%E0%B8%95%E0%B9%88%E0%B8%B3) displays Notice 14 as the operative geographic schedule. The Wage Committee's [June 2026 meeting record](https://www.mol.go.th/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2026/06/1.-3-69.pdf) says it unanimously deferred a 2026 general-rate change and notes that the hotel association **sought** a court stay of Notice 14 cl.2(1)'s THB400 hotel floor; the record reports a request for the Committee's defence, not a granted stay. Require an actual court order before changing that branch. The [15 September 2026 Ministry account](https://www.mol.go.th/news/%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%AD%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%9B%E0%B8%A5%E0%B8%B1%E0%B8%94%E0%B8%AF%E0%B9%81%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%99-%E0%B9%80%E0%B8%9B%E0%B9%87%E0%B8%99%E0%B8%9B%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%B0%E0%B8%98%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%99%E0%B8%81%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%9B%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%B0%E0%B8%8A%E0%B8%B8%E0%B8%A1%E0%B8%84%E0%B8%93%E0%B8%B0%E0%B8%AD%E0%B8%99%E0%B8%B8%E0%B8%81%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%A1%E0%B8%81%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%A7%E0%B8%B4%E0%B8%8A%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%81%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%A3%E0%B9%81%E0%B8%A5%E0%B8%B0%E0%B8%81%E0%B8%A5%E0%B8%B1%E0%B9%88%E0%B8%99%E0%B8%81%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%AD%E0%B8%87-%E0%B8%9B%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%B0%E0%B8%88%E0%B8%B3%E0%B8%9B%E0%B8%B5%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%9A%E0%B8%9B%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%B0%E0%B8%A1%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%93-%E0%B8%9E-%E0%B8%A8-2569) concerns proposals for **2027**, not an enacted 2026 replacement. The separate skill-standard index and Gazette Notice 15 are already captured in TH-SKILL-01/02. This index check does not establish that every Gazette provincial or sector notice has been enumerated; authenticated notice-by-notice amendment review is still open.
- **Social Security.** The Ministry's [11 September 2026 CARE announcement](https://www.mol.go.th/news/%E0%B8%88%E0%B8%B8%E0%B8%A5%E0%B8%9E%E0%B8%B1%E0%B8%99%E0%B8%98%E0%B9%8C-%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%A1%E0%B8%A7-%E0%B9%81%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%99-%E0%B9%80%E0%B8%9B%E0%B8%B4%E0%B8%94%E0%B8%81%E0%B8%B4%E0%B8%88%E0%B8%81%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%A1-run-for-care-%E0%B8%8A%E0%B8%A7%E0%B8%99%E0%B8%AD%E0%B8%AD%E0%B8%81%E0%B8%81%E0%B8%B3%E0%B8%A5%E0%B8%B1%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%81%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%A2%E0%B8%A7%E0%B8%B1%E0%B8%99%E0%B8%99%E0%B8%B5%E0%B9%89-%E0%B9%80%E0%B8%9E%E0%B8%B7%E0%B9%88%E0%B8%AD%E0%B8%9A%E0%B8%B3%E0%B8%99%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%8D%E0%B8%97%E0%B8%B5%E0%B9%88%E0%B8%A1%E0%B8%B1%E0%B9%88%E0%B8%99%E0%B8%84%E0%B8%87%E0%B9%83%E0%B8%99%E0%B8%A7%E0%B8%B1%E0%B8%99%E0%B8%AB%E0%B8%99%E0%B9%89%E0%B8%B2-%E0%B8%AA%E0%B8%A3%E0%B9%89%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%81%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%B1%E0%B8%9A%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%B9%E0%B9%89-%E0%B8%9A%E0%B8%B3%E0%B8%99%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%8D%E0%B8%8A%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%A0%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%9E%E0%B8%AA%E0%B8%B9%E0%B8%95%E0%B8%A3-care) explicitly says Cabinet approved only a **draft in principle** on 14 July and that it remained in the legal process. Do not substitute CARE for TH-SS-08's operative pension rule. Complete SSO/Gazette amendment review remains open.
- **SSO dental.** The [Medical Committee's 21 April 2026 Gazette notice](https://ratchakitcha.soc.go.th/documents/110491.pdf) replaces the dental-benefit schedule effective 1 May, creating TH-SS-10's provider/procedure route. Its recital visibly names an amendment dated **28 September 2026**, later than the signed publication. Treat that as an unresolved source-history anomaly requiring SSO/Gazette correction or clarification; do not infer an unseen September amendment or erase the operative May cutover.
- **Tax.** The Revenue Department's [2026 personal-return forms index](https://www.rd.go.th/68191.html), updated 1 July, lists advance/half-year forms but does not yet supply a final 2026 ภ.ง.ด.91 instruction set; its [2026 corporate-return forms index](https://rd.go.th/68605.html) is not a payroll PIT authority. TH-PIT-01/06 therefore retain the 2026 parameter census as open. Direct Code and Department orders expose the distinct housing, recipient-class and certificate branches now recorded in TH-PIT-07/09; the 2025 [Thai ESGX instructions](https://www.rd.go.th/fileadmin/tax_pdf/pit/2568/Ins94_050668.pdf) also impose the prior-purchase 2026 branch TH-PIT-10. The [2025 ภ.ง.ด.95](https://www.rd.go.th/fileadmin/tax_pdf/pit/2568/241268PIT95.pdf) and enacted [Decrees 641](https://www.rd.go.th/fileadmin/user_upload/kormor/newlaw/dc641.pdf), [674](https://www.rd.go.th/fileadmin/user_upload/kormor/newlaw/dc674.pdf) and [743](https://www.rd.go.th/fileadmin/user_upload/kormor/newlaw/dc743.pdf) establish three different conditional preferential **employment** regimes, now TH-PIT-11–16. The Finance Ministry's [2021 southern-border 3% skilled-worker measure](https://www.fpo.go.th/main/getattachment/News/Press-conference/14064/031-2564-%E0%B9%81%E0%B8%96%E0%B8%A5%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%82%E0%B9%88%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%A7-%E0%B8%81%E0%B8%84-%E0%B8%A1%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%95%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%81%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%A3%E0%B9%80%E0%B8%82%E0%B8%95%E0%B8%9E%E0%B8%B1%E0%B8%92%E0%B8%99%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%9E%E0%B8%B4%E0%B9%80%E0%B8%A8%E0%B8%A9%E0%B9%80%E0%B8%89%E0%B8%9E%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%B0%E0%B8%81%E0%B8%B4%E0%B8%88.pdf.aspx), s.3.3, expressly describes income received only through 31 December 2023; do not reuse it for 2025–26 merely because the separate [2025 southern-border return attachment](https://www.rd.go.th/fileadmin/tax_pdf/pit/2568/Ins90_241268.pdf) still covers section-40(7)/(8) business income. Check any later signed extension before treating the payroll 3% branch as current. The [2024 regional hiring/investment measure](https://www.rd.go.th/fileadmin/user_upload/kormor/newlaw/dg447A.pdf) is a business-tax incentive, not a 3% employee withholding selector.
- **Gazette OSH.** Three distinct notices and one licensing-training notice published 1 May 2026 take effect the next day: [continuing-development credit](https://ratchakitcha.soc.go.th/documents/113533.pdf), [expert work schedule/report forms](https://ratchakitcha.soc.go.th/documents/113532.pdf), [licence forms](https://ratchakitcha.soc.go.th/documents/113530.pdf) and [initial training/testing](https://ratchakitcha.soc.go.th/documents/113531.pdf). TH-SAFE-04/06 separate the licensed expert's duties from the employer's evidence of qualified engagement. The underlying 2024 licensing regulation and other sector OSH instruments still require a full applicability census.
- **OSH sector-regulation census.** The [DLPW downloads index](https://osh.labour.go.th/downloads/) separately indexes work at height, worker medical examinations/reporting, heat/light/noise, machines/cranes/boilers, construction, ionising radiation, dangerous chemicals, electricity, confined spaces, hazard assessment and fire prevention. TH-SAFE-09–11 now expose three distinct branches of the operative [2021 machinery regulation](https://osh.labour.go.th/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/machine-2564.pdf), and TH-SAFE-12 exposes the [2025 Act-scope exclusions](https://osh.labour.go.th/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/49135.pdf). Each remaining index category and its delegated notices need signed-text, amendment and applicability reconciliation before the TH HR denominator can close. DLPW's [2026 electricity-review meeting](https://osh.labour.go.th/%E0%B8%81%E0%B8%AD%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%84%E0%B8%A7%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%A1%E0%B8%9B%E0%B8%A5%E0%B8%AD%E0%B8%94%E0%B8%A0%E0%B8%B1%E0%B8%A2%E0%B9%81%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%99-%E0%B8%88-4/) and [chemical/dust drafting meeting](https://osh.labour.go.th/%E0%B8%81%E0%B8%AD%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%84%E0%B8%A7%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%A1%E0%B8%9B%E0%B8%A5%E0%B8%AD%E0%B8%94%E0%B8%A0%E0%B8%B1%E0%B8%A2%E0%B9%81%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%99-%E0%B8%88-53/) discuss drafts; neither establishes a new operative rule.
- **Special household work.** The [Ministry's Regulation No.15 summary](https://khonkaen.mol.go.th/wp-content/uploads/sites/59/2024/07/4%E0%B8%AA%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%B8%E0%B8%9B%E0%B8%AA%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%B0%E0%B8%AA%E0%B8%B3%E0%B8%84%E0%B8%B1%E0%B8%8D-%E0%B8%81%E0%B8%8E%E0%B8%81%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%B0%E0%B8%97%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%A7%E0%B8%87-%E0%B8%89%E0%B8%9A%E0%B8%B1%E0%B8%9A%E0%B8%97%E0%B8%B5%E0%B9%88-15-%E0%B8%9E.%E0%B8%A8.-2567.pdf) says minimum-wage coverage and 98/45 maternity; Notice 14's explanation item 9 does not list domestic work among its exclusions, while Act No.9 later changes the general maternity sections to 120/60. The signed household regulation and any post-2025 amendment/interpretation must be checked before selecting the domestic wage or maternity branch. TH-HR-17 stays unresolved; no generic exclusion or automatic 120/60 carry-through is certified.

### Source and coverage review — 2026-09-28

Nothing below is certified; every new row is `Implemented? No`.

- **Sources fixed.** TH-SS-01 and TH-SS-04 pointed at two misspelled Ministry PDF paths that returned a soft-404; both now use the working consolidated Social Security Act (through No.4 B.E.2558), and the [SSO mirror](https://www.sso.go.th/wpr/download/download_by_pool_file/14626) is added. No later enacted SSA amendment was found; CARE remains a draft.
- **Rate source added.** The 5%/5% s.33 rate now cites the signed 2565 contribution-rate regulation, Schedule B, from 1 January 2023; the "unresolved rounding" note is replaced by SSA s.46 (TH-SS-11).
- **Stale consolidation flagged.** Every citation of the Thai 2019 Ministry consolidation (`labour_protection_2541_new62.pdf`) now says it stops at Act No.7 B.E.2562 and predates Act No.8 B.E.2566 and No.9 B.E.2568. Whether No.8 or No.9 touched the cited sections was not checked. The English annotated PDF was already caveated.
- **Incorrect row corrected.** TH-DEDUCT-01 no longer says there is no TH cap: s.76 caps items (2)–(5) at 10% each and one-fifth combined.
- **New rows.** TH-WORK-04–08 (wage definition, s.68 divisor, OT/holiday multipliers and s.65 classes, hour limits, holidays); TH-LEAVE-05–07; TH-SS-11–13 (rounding, eligibility/filing/surcharge, contributory wage); TH-UNEMP-02 (force-majeure unemployment, B.E.2569 regulation dated 11 February 2026); TH-PIT-19–22 (residence, fund reliefs, Royal Decree No.805, MR No.401); TH-EXIT-02–07 (leave payout, severance ladder and retirement, notice, s.119, relocation, restructuring); TH-DEDUCT-02 (Student Loan Fund); TH-HR-29–30 (s.9 surcharge, payment channel and cadence). TH-PIT-04 now carries the MOF-notice due dates, with ภ.ง.ด.1ก due in February (8 March e-filed), not January.
- **Corroboration added.** The Ministry's unofficial English translation of Notice 14 (Notice 14 section). TH-SAFE-01's only statute link is a 2011 English translation; the controlling Thai text is still to be located.
- **Automation blockers.** Resolved 28 September 2026: `src/lib/statutory_sources.ts` carries a TH key (ratchakitcha.soc.go.th, www.ocs.go.th, www.mol.go.th, www.sso.go.th, www.rd.go.th, infocenter.oic.go.th), and statutory-drift.test 'every seeded jurisdiction has official sources to research' fails for any seeded jurisdiction without one. Candidate hosts already cited here: ratchakitcha.soc.go.th, www.ocs.go.th, www.mol.go.th, lb.mol.go.th, osh.labour.go.th, catalog.sso.go.th, infocenter.oic.go.th, www.rd.go.th, dfund.dep.go.th, www.dsd.go.th, www.prd.go.th, plus www.sso.go.th added today. On 28 September every cited ratchakitcha.soc.go.th Gazette PDF and thaigov.go.th page returned 403 to scripted fetches, and the Gazette home page also denied a Browser-pane session; www.mol.go.th served an Incapsula challenge to repeated scripted fetches. Re-verification therefore needs the Ministry, OCS, RD or SSO mirror of each instrument. The EWF 2031 rate step (TH-EWF-02) was unverified for this reason until Round 3 read the Gazette facsimile the Council of State serves.

### Round-2 closure — 2026-09-28

- **Gazette re-read.** ratchakitcha.soc.go.th still returns HTTP 403 to curl and WebFetch, and the Browser pane and Chrome extension refuse or cannot reach it. The s.33 base regulation B.E.2568 was read from the SSO's own copy ([download_by_pool_file/47755](https://www.sso.go.th/wpr/download/download_by_pool_file/47755); also linked from the [SSO information-centre index](https://infocenter.oic.go.th/%E0%B8%AA%E0%B8%B3%E0%B8%99%E0%B8%B1%E0%B8%81%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%99%E0%B8%9B%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%B0%E0%B8%81%E0%B8%B1%E0%B8%99%E0%B8%AA%E0%B8%B1%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%84%E0%B8%A1/topic.php?gid=8151&mid=1017)): signed 11 December 2025, Gazette vol.142 part 81 Kor p.5, 12 December 2025; cl.1 in force 1 January 2026; cl.2 repeals MR No.7 (B.E.2538); cl.3 the three steps above. The 2029 and 2032 steps are sealed as versions (every row cloned). The flood notice remained unread and the EWF rate regulation was read in Round 3 (see below).
- **Severance tax.** TH-PIT-05 is now priced by scheme SEVERANCE_TAX in every version (unchanged law, fixed in place): MR No.126 cl.2(51) exemption, then s.50(1) para.3 / s.48(5) for five years' service or more; an unresolved under-five-year remainder refuses the run.
- **ล.ย.01.** PIT carries election `ly01_deductions` (P.96 cl.1(2)) in every version (TH-PIT-06).
- **Special severance.** ss.120–122 read in the Krisdika consolidation and priced on the SEVERANCE_PAY line in every version (TH-EXIT-06/07); whether No.8 B.E.2566 or No.9 B.E.2568 touched ss.17–17/1 or ss.118–122 is not verified (both post-date the consolidation; the No.9 Gazette PDF is 403), though the register's reading of No.9 ss.3–9 names none of them.
- **Engine gaps closed the same day:** the Notice 14 daily floor landed for daily/hourly contracts (`wages.daily_by_worksite`, golden 'Notice 14 daily minimum wage…'; TH-WAGE-01), and s.17/1 notice pay is a NOTICE_IN_LIEU catalogue line (TH-EXIT-04); the golden carries no `test.todo`. Special severance stays on the SEVERANCE_PAY line: one line is the s.118 + ss.120–122 sum the law owes, and the matrix would list a split class as `[]` like severance.
- **Not resolved at Round 2:** the 30-day divisor readings (TH-WORK-05), the flood and EWF Gazette texts (EWF since read; see Round 3).

### Round-4 closure — 2026-09-28

Owner rule (decided 2026-09-28): where the law states a rule it is followed exactly; where it is silent, the most defensible lawful default is taken (literal text > regulator guidance > common practice), made a dated settings value where one exists, and the row's decision reason is closed.

- **TH-WORK-05** closed: monthly ÷ 30 (`ordinary_divisor_days` 30.0) for the day of a monthly wage outside s.68; calendar-day proration (`proration.by`). s.68 is the Act's only stated conversion and s.118's ladder is in 30-day months. Residual, law-stated rather than a decision: the s.23 seven-hour hazardous day.
- **TH-PIT-05** closed: an exit payment's taxable remainder with under five years' service is withheld on the PIT line as s.50(1) para.1's once-paid payment by the cl.1(5) difference method (every version, fixed in place; SEVERANCE_TAX's refusal removed). Goldens TAX-RETIRE-3Y 12,639.58 and NIL-3Y 1,664.58, hand-computed.
- **TH-WAGE-01** decided: daily rate × 30 for a monthly wage (every version's `wages.authority`); golden 'a monthly wage meets Notice 14 at the daily rate × 30' passes; the engine compares every pay frequency.
- **TH-SS-13** closed: s.5 read literally; bonus, reimbursement, severance, notice pay and s.67 leave pay are outside, each probed.
- **Retries, 28 Sep 2026:** ratchakitcha.soc.go.th/documents/100888.pdf still returns the site's "Access Denied" page in the Browser pane and HTTP 403 to curl; www.labour.go.th times out to curl and the Browser pane's navigation fails. The flood notice (TH-SS-02) stays on the register's transcription, corroborated by the PRD and press reports of the 8 January 2026 Gazette publication (secondary, not authority).

The signed [Labour Protection Act No.7](https://ratchakitcha.soc.go.th/documents/17076199.pdf) also amended current baseline branches that the older English consolidation does not capture: s.17/1 pay in lieu of the missing termination notice is due **on termination day**; s.34 necessary personal-business leave is **at least three working days per year** and s.57/1 pays at most three; s.53 requires equal wages and premium pay for work of equal nature, quality and quantity or equal value regardless of sex; s.70 requires wages/premiums at least monthly and termination amounts within **three days**; s.75 temporary non-force-majeure business suspension pays at least **75%** of prior ordinary wages; s.118 grants **400 days** of final wage at 20+ years (and 300 days for 10–<20 years); s.120 revises relocation notice and adds ss.120/1–120/2 order routes; s.9 includes **15% annual default interest** on specified unpaid items. The Act's old 98-day maternity/45-paid branch was replaced by No.9 from 7 December 2025 for its applicable workers. Each exact amount still needs a TH production probe and current amendment check; cross-employer consent/service and special-worker applicability must be resolved before output.

| TH-HR-24 | At an establishment with at least 50 employees, establish a welfare committee with at least five elected employee representatives, unless an existing labour-relations employee committee performs the role; consult it at least once every three months and on a justified majority/union request, and post mandatory or agreed welfare provisions. | Establishment-specific dated headcount, election or existing committee record, terms, meetings, requests, welfare rules and notices → valid committee, consultation and displayed welfare evidence. | [Labour Protection Act](https://searchlaw.ocs.go.th/council-of-state/#/public/doc/ZGN3NXk0eENvNjBSdjRnT2NsdjFTQT09) (Council of State consolidation through Act No.9 B.E.2568), ss.95–99; [Ministry welfare guidance](https://www.mol.go.th/employee/%E0%B8%AA%E0%B8%A7%E0%B8%B1%E0%B8%AA%E0%B8%94%E0%B8%B4%E0%B8%81%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%A3%E0%B9%81%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%99). | Open/External: 49/50 workers, elected versus existing representative body, quarterly meetings, triggered request and applicable s.95 welfare regulations; reconcile current election notice. | establishment/headcount/meeting date; from 1 Dec 2025 | No [EXTERNAL 2026-09-29] | No establishment welfare-committee election, meeting/request or posting evidence; human organisational duty. |

| TH-DIS-04 | Do not impose a rule, pay decision, recruitment or workplace practice that unfairly discriminates against a worker with a disability, including an apparently neutral rule that removes a disability-related benefit; review permitted objective grounds and feasible remedial support. | Job requirements, decision criteria, disability-related request, workplace access, comparators and response → documented fair decision or corrective action. | [Disability Empowerment Act, as amended](https://dfund.dep.go.th/wp-content/uploads/2023/02/1.%E0%B8%9E%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%B0%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%8A%E0%B8%9A%E0%B8%B1%E0%B8%8D%E0%B8%8D%E0%B8%B1%E0%B8%95%E0%B8%B4%E0%B8%AA%E0%B9%88%E0%B8%87%E0%B9%80%E0%B8%AA%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%B4%E0%B8%A1%E0%B9%81%E0%B8%A5%E0%B8%B0%E0%B8%9E%E0%B8%B1%E0%B8%92%E0%B8%99%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%84%E0%B8%B8%E0%B8%93%E0%B8%A0%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%9E%E0%B8%8A%E0%B8%B5%E0%B8%A7%E0%B8%B4%E0%B8%95%E0%B8%84%E0%B8%99%E0%B8%9E%E0%B8%B4%E0%B8%81%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%A3-%E0%B8%9E.%E0%B8%A8.-2550-%E0%B9%81%E0%B8%A5%E0%B8%B0%E0%B9%81%E0%B8%81%E0%B9%89%E0%B9%84%E0%B8%82%E0%B9%80%E0%B8%9E%E0%B8%B4%E0%B9%88%E0%B8%A1%E0%B9%80%E0%B8%95%E0%B8%B4%E0%B8%A1%E0%B8%89%E0%B8%9A%E0%B8%B1%E0%B8%9A%E0%B8%97%E0%B8%B5%E0%B9%88-2-%E0%B8%9E.%E0%B8%A8.-2556.pdf), ss.15–16. | Open/External: direct and indirect discrimination, objectively justified exception and feasible remediation; review current subsidiary accessibility rules by workplace type. | recruitment, employment and pay decisions; from 1 Dec 2025 | No | No evidence-backed disability discrimination review, decision control or remedy record; human duty. |

| TH-DIS-05 | Assess 7.5% annual interest on unpaid disability-fund debt from the legally late date: 1 April after the 31 March annual s.34 deadline, or for an uncured midyear vacancy from the vacancy date if later than 1 April; the 45-day replacement window does not erase shortfall days if missed. | Annual due/paid dates, vacancy and replacement dates, uncovered daily amount and payment history → separately itemised interest and receipt. | [Disability Empowerment Act s.34 para.2](https://dfund.dep.go.th/wp-content/uploads/2023/02/1.%E0%B8%9E%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%B0%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%8A%E0%B8%9A%E0%B8%B1%E0%B8%8D%E0%B8%8D%E0%B8%B1%E0%B8%95%E0%B8%B4%E0%B8%AA%E0%B9%88%E0%B8%87%E0%B9%80%E0%B8%AA%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%B4%E0%B8%A1%E0%B9%81%E0%B8%A5%E0%B8%B0%E0%B8%9E%E0%B8%B1%E0%B8%92%E0%B8%99%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%84%E0%B8%B8%E0%B8%93%E0%B8%A0%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%9E%E0%B8%8A%E0%B8%B5%E0%B8%A7%E0%B8%B4%E0%B8%95%E0%B8%84%E0%B8%99%E0%B8%9E%E0%B8%B4%E0%B8%81%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%A3-%E0%B8%9E.%E0%B8%A8.-2550-%E0%B9%81%E0%B8%A5%E0%B8%B0%E0%B9%81%E0%B8%81%E0%B9%89%E0%B9%84%E0%B8%82%E0%B9%80%E0%B8%9E%E0%B8%B4%E0%B9%88%E0%B8%A1%E0%B9%80%E0%B8%95%E0%B8%B4%E0%B8%A1%E0%B8%89%E0%B8%9A%E0%B8%B1%E0%B8%9A%E0%B8%97%E0%B8%B5%E0%B9%88-2-%E0%B8%9E.%E0%B8%A8.-2556.pdf); [2017 regulation cl.5](https://www.mol.go.th/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2018/02/313.pdf); [DEP employer manual](https://dfund.dep.go.th/wp-content/uploads/2023/12/%E0%B8%84%E0%B8%B9%E0%B9%88%E0%B8%A1%E0%B8%B7%E0%B8%AD%E0%B8%88%E0%B9%89%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%99-%E0%B9%80%E0%B8%AD%E0%B8%81%E0%B8%8A%E0%B8%99.pdf), pp. on interest. | Open/External: March 31/April 1 payment, April 20 vacancy replaced on day 45/46, partial payment and refund; DEP FAQ Q25 says 1 March contrary to its Q29, the signed 31 March due date and DEP manual, so Q25 is a guidance error, not an earlier statutory interest start. | employer/debt/payment date; from 1 Dec 2025 | No | No annual/daily shortfall ledger, interest assessment, payment/reversal or authority receipt. |

### Workmen's Compensation Fund branch detail and R49 evidence migration

The signed [2018 amendment](https://www.mol.go.th/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2021/02/3-%E0%B8%9E.%E0%B8%A3.%E0%B8%9A.%E0%B9%80%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%B4%E0%B8%99%E0%B8%97%E0%B8%94%E0%B9%81%E0%B8%97%E0%B8%99-%E0%B8%89%E0%B8%9A%E0%B8%B1%E0%B8%9A%E0%B8%97%E0%B8%B5%E0%B9%88-2-%E0%B8%9E.%E0%B8%A8.-2561.pdf), [updated Act](https://www.mol.go.th/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2021/02/1-%E0%B8%9E.%E0%B8%A3.%E0%B8%9A.%E0%B9%80%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%B4%E0%B8%99%E0%B8%97%E0%B8%94%E0%B9%81%E0%B8%97%E0%B8%99-%E0%B8%9E.%E0%B8%A8.-2537-%E0%B8%89%E0%B8%9A%E0%B8%B1%E0%B8%9A-update.pdf) and [2019 assessment notice](https://www.mol.go.th/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2021/02/6-%E0%B8%9B%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%B0%E0%B8%81%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%A8%E0%B8%81%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%B0%E0%B8%97%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%A7%E0%B8%87%E0%B9%81%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%99-%E0%B9%80%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%B7%E0%B9%88%E0%B8%AD%E0%B8%87-%E0%B8%9B%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%B0%E0%B9%80%E0%B8%A0%E0%B8%97-%E0%B8%82%E0%B8%99%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%94%E0%B8%82%E0%B8%AD%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%81%E0%B8%B4%E0%B8%88%E0%B8%81%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%A3-%E0%B8%97%E0%B9%89%E0%B8%AD%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%97%E0%B8%B5%E0%B9%88%E0%B8%97%E0%B8%B5%E0%B9%88%E0%B9%83%E0%B8%AB%E0%B9%89%E0%B8%99%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%A2%E0%B8%88%E0%B9%89%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%88%E0%B9%88%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%A2%E0%B9%80%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%B4%E0%B8%99%E0%B8%AA%E0%B8%A1%E0%B8%97%E0%B8%9A%E0%B8%AF.pdf) control the following still-open subbranches. The table above groups them; this detail retains the original R49 `TH-WCF01`–`TH-WCF08` trace identifiers so the shared tracker can eventually be retired without losing tests or evidence.

| R49 finding → register             | Exact branch, required input/evidence and open case                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | Implemented? | If not, why?                                                                                                          |
| ---------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `TH-WCF01` → TH-WCF-01             | Amended Act s.4 and 2019 notice cl.5: covered employer with **at least one worker** anywhere in Thailand; separately inspect government, state-enterprise, foreign-government/international-body, ministerially excluded, nonbusiness natural-person household and itinerant/stall trade categories. A blanket nonprofit or government exemption is unsupported.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | No           | No TH employer/worker liability selector or complete exclusion amendments.                                            |
| `TH-WCF02` → TH-WCF-03             | Amended s.44: register and pay within **30 days** of liability, report changed facts by the **15th next month**; [SSO forms notice](https://infocenter.oic.go.th/FILEWEB/CABINFOCENTER66/DRAWER004/GENERAL/DATA0000/00000169.PDF) prescribes สปส.1-01 and สปส.6-15 and office/electronic routes. The old 30-day change instruction is superseded.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | No           | Registration/change event and receipt evidence absent.                                                                |
| `TH-WCF03` → TH-WCF-01, TH-WCF-02  | Act s.45 and notice cls.6–11: SSO-assigned one of **1,091** five-digit activity codes, mixed-activity rule, corrected-code assessment; each worker's **annual** assessable wages capped THB240,000 and prorated for service shorter than a year. Whole-baht contribution rounds 50 satang up. Wage means money for normal-day/hour time or output, including paid holiday/leave regardless of component label. A 26 September index extraction found serials 1–1,091, first code `01111`, last `99009`, with no missing serial/duplicate code; **rate cells and later amendments remain unverified**.                                                                                                                                                                            | No           | Full rate transcription/verification, capped worker-year base and annual engine absent.                               |
| `TH-WCF04` → TH-WCF-02, TH-WCF-03  | Notice cls.13–15: January estimated annual payment; February actual prior-year wage report/difference; March added-contribution payment; cessation report within 30 days; separate 30-day settlement after code/wage assessment notice. Preserve estimate, assessment, payment, refund and correction dates.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | No           | Annual settlement/receipt ledger and later deadline amendments unverified.                                            |
| `TH-WCF05` → TH-WCF-02             | Notice cls.16–19: approved quarterly instalments require **25% deposit**; experience rating after four consecutive years uses Table 2's −50% to +50% basic-rate adjustment (70.01%–80.00% unchanged). The [2022 SSO loss-ratio notice](https://ratchakitcha.soc.go.th/documents/17232834.pdf) superseded its 2019 predecessor from 1 January 2023: printed `0.21 × previous-year ratio + 0.70 × applicable average`, first experience year uses three preceding calendar years, later years carry the prior calculated average, result capped 200; older arising contributions retain older method. Do **not** normalise printed coefficients: 100 and 100 yield 91. Probe first/later year, cap and 30/30.01, 70/70.01, 80/80.01, 120/120.01 band edges against SSO assessment. | No           | Historical fund loss/claim ledger, office rate and experience selector absent.                                        |
| `TH-WCF06` → TH-WCF-03             | Amended s.46: late/incomplete charge **2% monthly**, capped at unpaid contribution; locality relief notices can reduce it. Amending s.31 preserves the former 3% only for arrears already overdue **before** the 2018 commencement.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | No           | Arrears history, partial-month interpretation, local relief and late-charge path absent.                              |
| `TH-WCF07` → TH-WCF-04, TH-SAFE-03 | Amended ss.13, 18–19, 48: immediate treatment, SSO report within **15 days of knowledge** on กท.16, and distinct compensation outcomes, including **70% of defined monthly wages** for specified incapacity/death/disappearance subject to duration/minima/maxima. [SSO incident forms](https://infocenter.oic.go.th/FILEWEB/CABINFOCENTER66/DRAWER004/GENERAL/DATA0000/00000170.PDF) permit office/post/electronic filing. Old 60%/three-day-wait guidance is superseded. The separate OSH s.34 safety-inspector report remains due.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | No           | Incident, treatment, insurer-benefit order and both filing receipts absent; current implementing benefit limits open. |
| `TH-WCF08` → TH-WCF-05             | Act ss.49–52: normally **180-day claim**; post-employment occupational illness has **two years from discovery**; SSO order specifies amount/period and employer payment within **seven days of notice**; changed condition/facts may create a later order; written appeal normally within **30 days**, except s.47 order; s.25 has a fund-on-behalf payer route. Forms กท.16 or กท.16/1 and medical proof are different evidence paths.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | No           | Claim/order/appeal and claimant/beneficiary lifecycle absent.                                                         |

The [old English Act](https://www.mol.go.th/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2021/02/Compensation-Act-B.E.2537.pdf), [undated Ministry Ratchaburi page](https://www.mol.go.th/en/%E0%B8%81%E0%B8%AD%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%97%E0%B8%B8%E0%B8%99%E0%B9%80%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%B4%E0%B8%99%E0%B8%97%E0%B8%94%E0%B9%81%E0%B8%97%E0%B8%99-2) and [Ministry brochure](https://www.mol.go.th/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2021/02/1-%E0%B9%81%E0%B8%9C%E0%B9%88%E0%B8%99%E0%B8%9E%E0%B8%B1%E0%B8%9A%E0%B8%81%E0%B8%AD%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%97%E0%B8%B8%E0%B8%99%E0%B9%80%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%B4%E0%B8%99%E0%B8%97%E0%B8%94%E0%B9%81%E0%B8%97%E0%B8%99-TH.pdf) are corroboration or superseded explanations, not the rate source. A [July 2026 Ministry report](https://www.mol.go.th/news/611420) described a **draft** assessment amendment and a [September consultation](https://www.mol.go.th/news/%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%AD%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%9B%E0%B8%A5%E0%B8%B1%E0%B8%94%E0%B8%AF-%E0%B9%81%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%99-%E0%B8%A3%E0%B9%88%E0%B8%A7%E0%B8%A1%E0%B9%80%E0%B8%9B%E0%B8%B4%E0%B8%94%E0%B9%80%E0%B8%A7%E0%B8%97%E0%B8%B5%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%B1%E0%B8%9A%E0%B8%9F%E0%B8%B1%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%84%E0%B8%A7%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%A1%E0%B8%84%E0%B8%B4%E0%B8%94%E0%B9%80%E0%B8%AB%E0%B9%87%E0%B8%99-%E0%B8%A3%E0%B9%88%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%87-%E0%B8%9E-%E0%B8%A3-%E0%B8%9A-%E0%B9%80%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%B4%E0%B8%99%E0%B8%97%E0%B8%94%E0%B9%81%E0%B8%97%E0%B8%99-%E0%B8%A1%E0%B8%B8%E0%B9%88%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%A2%E0%B8%81%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%B0%E0%B8%94%E0%B8%B1%E0%B8%9A%E0%B8%84%E0%B8%A7%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%A1%E0%B8%84%E0%B8%B8%E0%B9%89%E0%B8%A1%E0%B8%84%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%AD%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%A5%E0%B8%B9%E0%B8%81%E0%B8%88%E0%B9%89%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%87%E0%B9%83%E0%B8%AB%E0%B9%89%E0%B8%AA%E0%B8%AD%E0%B8%94%E0%B8%84%E0%B8%A5%E0%B9%89%E0%B8%AD%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%9A%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%B4%E0%B8%9A%E0%B8%97%E0%B8%9B%E0%B8%B1%E0%B8%88%E0%B8%88%E0%B8%B8%E0%B8%9A%E0%B8%B1%E0%B8%99) followed. Neither is a published new rate. Gazette/Ministry register recheck remains open.

### Other R49 branch detail retained for certification

| `TH-SW03` → TH-SKILL-02 | Notice 15 cl.5 applies to actual duties requiring **all or part** of the specified skill, even without a certificate.                   | No           | Dated duties/evidence selector and partial-match case absent.                                |

| `TH-SW05` → TH-SKILL-02 | Notice 15 commences after ninety days from Gazette publication on 16 June 2026; 14 September is a reasoned but unconfirmed application. | No           | Ministry annotated cutover and 13/14/15 September probes absent.                             |
| `TH-SW06` → TH-SKILL-03 | Student THB40/hour notice is advisory; qualification and binding geographic/skill floor remain separate.                                | No           | Current register, agency-description conflict and worker-status probes open.                 |

- **Skill standards (`TH-SW01`–`TH-SW05` → TH-SKILL-01/02).** [Ministry Notice 13](https://www.mol.go.th/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2024/01/%E0%B8%9B%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%B0%E0%B8%81%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%A8%E0%B8%AD%E0%B8%B1%E0%B8%95%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%84%E0%B9%88%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%88%E0%B9%89%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%95%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%A1%E0%B8%A1%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%95%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%90%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%99%E0%B8%9D%E0%B8%B5%E0%B8%A1%E0%B8%B7%E0%B8%AD-%E0%B8%89%E0%B8%9A%E0%B8%B1%E0%B8%9A%E0%B8%97%E0%B8%B5%E0%B9%88-13-%E0%B9%81%E0%B8%A5%E0%B8%B0%E0%B8%84%E0%B8%B3%E0%B8%8A%E0%B8%B5%E0%B9%89%E0%B9%81%E0%B8%88%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%9B%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%B0%E0%B8%81%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%A8-%E0%B8%AF-2.pdf) covers 129 classifications from 18 March 2024; [Gazette Notice 14](https://ratchakitcha.soc.go.th/documents/73261.pdf) adds 13 rate entries, and the [Ministry's 2025 report](https://www.mol.go.th/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2025/12/%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%A2%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%99%E0%B8%AA%E0%B8%96%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%99%E0%B8%81%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%93%E0%B9%8C%E0%B8%94%E0%B9%89%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%99%E0%B9%81%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%99-Q3Y68.pdf) says 141 **distinct** occupations across the two, so 129+13 is not a distinct count. [Notice 15](https://ratchakitcha.soc.go.th/documents/117083.pdf) adds flux-cored and MIG welders grades 1/2 (THB550/650 each), off-grid solar installer grade 1 (THB560) and industrial robot maintenance technician grade 1 (THB630). Its cl.5 covers a position requiring **all or part** of the specified skill; cl.6 supplies a certificate-receipt start for a tested worker, so duties and receipt are separate triggers. A [Council of State construction opinion](https://comment.ocs.go.th/main/download/dlf/pdf/%E0%B9%80%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%B7%E0%B9%88%E0%B8%AD%E0%B8%87%E0%B9%80%E0%B8%AA%E0%B8%A3%E0%B9%87%E0%B8%88%E0%B8%97%E0%B8%B5%E0%B9%88%20290-2543) applied to 16 June 2026 publication implies 14 September start, but that is an inference to confirm against the Ministry's annotated notice; its [20 May announcement](https://www.mol.go.th/news/600298) alone cannot set commencement. **Implemented? No. Why?** Full rate/grade transcription, date/duties selector and TH production probes are absent. Probe 13/14/15 September, both welder grades, partial duty without certificate, and certificate received midperiod.
- **Student notice (`TH-SW06` → TH-SKILL-03).** [Signed Notice 2](https://infocenter.oic.go.th/FILEWEB/CABINFOCENTER3/DRAWER052/GENERAL/DATA0000/00000536.PDF), cls.3–5, describes THB40/hour as **advice** for qualifying age-15+ part-time students, excluding course-required placements and ordinary full-time jobs; the [Ministry explanation](https://www.mol.go.th/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2019/03/Datalabour_MOL_for03April2013.pdf), pp.23–24, concurs. A [2026 Government PRD announcement](https://www.prd.go.th/th/content/category/detail/id/39/iid/489718) calls it a minimum; this conflicts with the signed notice and is not itself an amendment. **Implemented? No. Why?** Current notice register, employment status and Notice 14/skill interaction unresolved. Probe employed four-hour student, genuine placement and otherwise applicable skill floor; never infer a zero or THB40 binding floor from `INTERN`/`STUDENT` alone.
- **EWF (`TH-EWF-01`–03).** [Commencement decree](https://ratchakitcha.soc.go.th/documents/84794.pdf) and [rate regulation](https://ratchakitcha.soc.go.th/documents/86102.pdf) start 1 October 2026: 0.25% each side through September 2031, then 0.50% each (rate regulation B.E.2568 cl.3, read 28 Sep 2026 from the [Council of State PDF](https://www.ocs.go.th/searchlaw/power_link/download/eyJ1dWlkIjoiNDNjMTc4OTgtODFiMS00MmVhLWIwNmYtMjQ4YzM1NzlmMzFhIiwiZmlsZW5hbWUiOiLguIQwMDc3IOC4geC4juC4geC4o-C4sOC4l-C4o-C4p-C4hyAyNTY4LTYw4LiBLTMucGRmIn0.CH4M5nGGlUmG48thQPthO-j_qpRZ-5GpRKA0pnN9pw8) of the Gazette pages; both rate steps are sealed and probed, TH-EWF-02). The [2024 alternative-arrangement regulation](https://www.ocs.go.th/searchlaw/law-index/item/13230) and its [2025 date amendment](https://ratchakitcha.soc.go.th/documents/86101.pdf) require actual employee-level exit/death welfare, written rates/collection/account inspection and separate member accounts; a company-wide provident-fund label alone does not exempt everyone. The [remittance regulation](https://ratchakitcha.soc.go.th/documents/51462.pdf) prescribes each-pay deduction, next-month **15th** list/payment, deemed contribution on unpaid wages, employer liability for a missed deduction, **5% monthly** late addition with a 15-day partial-month rule, and correction/refund. Its printed 2025 start conflicts with the delayed 2026 collection decree; current amendment/reconciliation remains open. The [Council of State index](https://www.ocs.go.th/searchlaw/law-index/item/13219), [August department notice](https://www.thaigov.go.th/th/news/167291) and [September e-Service notice](https://www.thaigov.go.th/th/news/168778) corroborate dates/administration, not missing legal branches. **Implemented? No. Why?** Member-by-member exemption, statutory wage, rounding, collection/deadline and receipts are not in a TH profile. Probe headcount 9/10, covered/uncovered members, September/October 2026 and 2031, THB20,000→THB50 per side, unpaid wage and missed deduction.
- **Insured maternity history (`TH-SS-03`).** The [Ministry's updated Social Security Act](https://www.mol.go.th/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2021/02/1-%E0%B8%9E%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%9A.%E0%B8%9B%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%B0%E0%B8%81%E0%B8%B1%E0%B8%99%E0%B8%AA%E0%B8%B1%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%84%E0%B8%A1-update.pdf), ss.57, 65, 67, requires **five of prior fifteen** insured contribution months, selects the highest three **insured wage bases**, divides by 90 and pays 50% for ninety days, at most twice. The [older e-Labour page](https://lb.mol.go.th/en/maternity) says seven months and is stale against the amended statute. Earlier employers' gross salaries cannot substitute for member-wide insured bases. **Implemented? No. Why?** No TH member-history path or production benefit probe. Probe five documented months across two employers with Oct–Dec 2025 bases THB10,000/12,000/15,000 and Jan–Feb 2026 THB17,500 each, versus only four months.
- **Unemployment (`TH-UNEMP-01`).** The [27 June 2025 Gazette amendment](https://ratchakitcha.soc.go.th/documents/75954.pdf) starts 28 June and raises eligible dismissal benefit to 60% of statutory daily wage for up to 180 days per occurrence. [Government PRD guidance](https://www.prd.go.th/th/content/category/detail/id/39/iid/406186) describes six contributions in fifteen, an eight-day wait, registration within thirty days, monthly reporting and exclusions; those administrative details and annual aggregate wording still require enacted-rule reconciliation. This is insurer-paid and distinct from employer severance. **Implemented? No. Why?** No TH insured-history entitlement or agency claim path. Probe dismissal/resignation, five/six insured months, 180/181 days and prior-employer bases.
- **Tax method (`TH-PIT-01`–06).** The [Revenue Department's 2025 instructions](https://www.rd.go.th/fileadmin/tax_pdf/pit/2568/Ins91_241268.pdf) give salary expense 50% capped THB100,000, THB60,000 personal allowance and net-income bands 0–150k exempt, 150–300k 5%, 300–500k 10%, 500–750k 15%, 750k–1m 20%, 1m–2m 25%, 2m–5m 30%, above 5m 35%. THB360,000 salary with only those two deductions gives THB200,000 taxable and THB2,500 annual tax. This is **2025**, not automatically every 2026 parameter. [Order P.96/2543](https://www.rd.go.th/3558.html) and [Order P.16/2530](https://www.rd.go.th/3613.html) require 12/24/52 annualisation, actual remaining pay occasions for a midyear hire, re-estimation after pay/relief changes, bonus incremental-tax difference, cumulative irregular-pay credit, full versus fixed employer-borne tax, final fractional remainder and December/final-employment reconciliation. [RD payroll system guidance](https://www.rd.go.th/62974/%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%B0%E0%B8%9A%E0%B8%9A%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%99%E0%B8%A0%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%A9%E0%B8%B5%E0%B9%80%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%B4%E0%B8%99%E0%B9%84%E0%B8%94%E0%B9%89%E0%B8%AB%E0%B8%B1%E0%B8%81%E0%B8%A0%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%A9%E0%B8%B5-%E0%B8%93-%E0%B8%97%E0%B8%B5%E0%B9%88%E0%B8%88%E0%B9%88%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%A2.html) and [electronic filing service](https://www.rd.go.th/62977.html) identify monthly ภ.ง.ด.1 and annual ภ.ง.ด.1ก; month-specific extensions and annual deadline remain to verify. **Implemented? No. Why?** TH relief/withholding and matching filing snapshots/probes are absent. Probe every tax-band edge, December one/two-date hire, bonus, changed relief, borne tax, corrected month and annual totals.

### Shared-tracker migration reconciliation

This is the destination for Thailand-applicable findings and evidence in the former consolidated tracker. The tracker remains until all nine registers pass the same reconciliation. A source review, arithmetic check or inventory row is **not** a passed production probe.

No TH profile.                                               |

New rows identify known branches but subsidiary-law census is still open; the number of rows is **not** an exhaustive denominator or compliance percentage.                       |

Test TH continuous/flexible unit, crossing leave, certificate/relationship persistence and member-history branches once a TH rule exists; no TH proof yet. |
