Its ten Decimal checks related to CN, not TH.                                                                                                    |

The Act No.9 cutover is independently corroborated by [Government PRD](https://thailand.prd.go.th/en/content/category/detail/id/48/iid/452044). A [National Human Rights Commission catalogue entry](https://www.nhrc.or.th/th/km/labour) gives a conflicting day-after-publication description; enacted s.2's **30-day** clause governs, yielding 7 December 2025. The [Labour Department guard announcement](https://www.thaigov.go.th/th/news/163271) corroborates the 24 April 2026 guard change but does not replace the Gazette text. The old WCF and insurance guidance conflicts are documented in the branch detail. These secondary sources are retained for audit provenance, not substituted for controlling law.

**Remaining denominator work:** enumerate every enacted Labour Protection Act amendment and delegated regulation applicable to each establishment, occupation, worker and period; enumerate all current Social Security, WCF, Revenue, EWF, Skill Development Fund, OSH, labour-relations and foreign-worker instruments; transcribe full skill and WCF rate schedules; classify insurer benefit versus employer payroll versus agency filing; verify every effective date and regional/sector exception; then add independently expected input/output, rejection and reversal probes for every branch. Certification is **No** until each row has source, applicable profile, production mechanism, saved result and external receipt where required.

## Verification contract and unresolved capabilities

For every in-product row: (1) verify the complete amendment history and effective interval; (2) encode the selector on a sealed TH version with sufficient fact/evidence fields; (3) execute boundary and adverse-input probes against production functions; (4) save, reverse and reproduce money outputs; (5) reconcile a filing/remittance receipt when the product claims to perform that external act, otherwise document the legal boundary. A passing source transcription test alone does not prove a payroll mechanism.

Eight sealed TH settings versions now cover 1 December 2025 onward. Their Notice 14 `wages.daily_by_worksite` and `daily_by_sector` tables feed `dailyFloorIssues`, which compares each measured ordinary workday against the applicable full-day place/sector floor across daily, hourly, weekly and monthly pay and blocks a shortfall or unknown worksite. This does not close the broader Thailand profile: sector and worker exclusions still need evidence and boundary probes; there is no skill-standard occupation/grade wage floor, annual WCF assessment/true-up, or occupational-incident and statutory-order lifecycle. Saved/reversal and production-path evidence remains open, so Thailand certification is **open**, with no defensible numerator or percentage.

### Shared implementation handoff, in dependency order

1. **Dated daily wage selection.** Landed 28 September 2026: the sealed Notice 14 place/sector tables, effective-dated employment-term worksite and sector, per-work-day worksite override, and `dailyFloorIssues` compare each normal workday to its full floor; a short day does not reduce it. The comparator covers daily/hourly and monthly/semi-monthly/weekly pay (owner rule: month ÷ `ordinary_divisor_days`, weekly × 52 ÷ 12 before the divisor), blocks missing or ambiguous worksite, and has boundary goldens for Chiang Mai district, Ko Samui, hotel type 2, a four-hour day, monthly rounding and a mid-period worksite transfer. Retained gaps: a work-day site override does not carry a work-day sector override, sector/classification and statutory exclusions lack a complete evidence census, and the saved/reversal/production path is not certified. The company-wide monthly `wages.by_region` table is not the Notice 14 comparator.
2. **Skill-standard wage selection.** Add a versioned occupation/grade table to the sealed law and dated worker facts for actual duties, matching standard, grade, certificate and employer-receipt date. A partially matching duty invokes Notice 15 cl.5 independently of a certificate; cl.6 supplies a separate receipt-date route. Compare the applicable skill daily floor alongside the geographic/sector floor, without averaging away a deficient workday. Refuse a missing grade where duties trigger the standard. Acceptance probe after confirmed Notice 15 commencement: grade-2 welder paid THB600 blocks below THB650 in a THB400 province, while grade-1 THB600 passes its THB550 skill floor; test a certificate received mid-period and a partial-duty match without one.
3. **Annual WCF ledger.** Add employer/activity risk classification, an office-confirmed basic/experience rate with evidence, capped **worker-year** wage accumulation and annual estimate/actual declaration/settlement. Store assessed amount, notices, payments, credits, refunds and reversals with event dates. This cannot be faked as a monthly `statutory_contributions` row. Acceptance probe: SSO-assigned 0.2% on a worker's THB300,000 annual covered wages assesses THB480 after the THB240,000 worker cap; THB10,250 assesses THB21 after whole-baht rounding. Correct a wage after settlement and verify the original assessment and credit remain reproducible.
4. **Incident and external-duty records.** Capture occupational incident, knowledge date, treatment, claim/order and payer facts where they affect in-product classification, payroll or warnings. When the product reports a filing or remittance as complete, retain the authority receipt and correction history; otherwise show the employer-facing legal boundary without claiming that the external act was performed.

### TH-S1 citation fix — 2026-09-28

- **Stale SSA copy replaced.** The SSO PDF (`download_by_pool_file/14626`) cited for ss.5, 33, 34, 44, 46, 47, 49 lists amendments only through No.3 B.E.2542. Every seed version (authority, obligations, sources), the golden header and TH-SS-01/-04/-11/-12/-13 now cite the [Council of State current consolidation](https://searchlaw.ocs.go.th/council-of-state/#/public/doc/alJWY29wVXFRUUo0WkF2MTEwSndpQT09) (footnotes through Gazette vol.139 part 66 Kor, 25 October 2565). Re-read against it: ss.5 (ค่าจ้าง), 33, 46 para.5 and 47 para.1–2 say what the seed prices; no figure moves.
- **Transcription fixed in place (every version).** s.49 para.1 as amended by No.4 B.E.2558 (in force before every sealed version): 2% a month from the day after the due date, capped at the contribution owed; the old "part-month of 15 days counts as a month" rule was repealed. The engine does not price the surcharge (SSO_MONTHLY_REMITTANCE stays PARTIAL); the obligation text and TH-SS-12 now state the current rule. s.47 para.1 now deems the insured paid from the wage due date, not the deduction date.
- **Golden:** 'SSO law is read from the current consolidation' — 12,345 → 617, 13,999 → 700 each side (s.46 para.5 rounding), and every version cites the consolidation.

### TH-S2 LPA consolidation replaced — 2026-09-28

- **Stale LPA copy replaced.** The Ministry's 2019 PDF (`labour_protection_2541_new62.pdf`, through No.7 B.E.2562; behind an Incapsula challenge to scripted fetches) is gone from every seed version (work rules, exit obligations, sources, EWF authority, severance and notice authorities), the golden header and every register row. They now cite the [Council of State current consolidation](https://searchlaw.ocs.go.th/council-of-state/#/public/doc/ZGN3NXk0eENvNjBSdjRnT2NsdjFTQT09), read in the Browser pane on 28 September 2026. Its footnotes run through No.9 B.E.2568 (Gazette vol.142 part 74 Kor p.41, 7 November 2025; s.2 in force 30 days after publication, so 7 December 2025) and No.8 B.E.2566 (vol.140 part 20 Kor p.56, 19 March 2023).
- **What No.8 and No.9 touched.** The consolidation's footnotes: No.8 added s.23/1 (work away from the workplace) only; No.9 added s.4/1, amended s.41 para.1, added s.41 para.4, s.41/1, s.59–59/2 and amended s.115/1 para.1. None of ss.5, 9, 17, 17/1, 22–30 (other than s.23/1), 32–36, 39/1, 47–48, 52, 54–58, 61–68, 70, 72, 76–77, 87–90, 95–99, 118–122 or 130–131 was amended, so the earlier "whether No.8 or No.9 touched…" caveats are resolved: no figure moves. Re-read against it: s.118 (30/90/180/240/300/400 days), s.119, s.122 (15 days a year, 360 cap), s.30, s.68, s.76 (10% each, one-fifth combined) and s.130 say what the seed prices. The consolidation reproduces only s.2 and the note of No.9, so no No.9 transitional section was found (TH-LEAVE-01's crossing-7-December question stays open).
- **Transcription fixed in place (every version).** The child-care leave authority cited "s.49/1"; the section is s.59/1 (50% of wages). Both No.9 child leaves are "not more than fifteen days" (ไม่เกินสิบห้าวัน), not fifteen working days. TH-LEAVE-01's source cell now names ss.41 and 59–59/2.
- **Payroll fix.** `leave.day_index` / `month_index` counted from each entry's first charge. Payroll refuses an entry that straddles a window, so a 120-day maternity leave is filed as several entries, and each restarted at day 1: every day was paid, not the first 60. It now counts from the first charged day of the event (entries of the code naming the same event date). An entry naming no event is unchanged.
- **Calendar-day charging now guarded.** The leave planner charges every date in a maternity span, including published holidays, REST/OFF and days without a roster code; the captured shift is nullable for those days. Payroll rejects missing or fractional dates, including gaps between entries of the same birth event. Daily- and hourly-paid Thai maternity earns the normal day wage on a non-WORK date, then the event's day-index rule removes the unpaid portion after day 60. Piece-rate maternity refuses pending the preceding wage-period average required by s.60. The March golden now exercises monthly, daily and hourly pay; the planner grammar probes holiday, rest and unrostered dates.
- **Golden:** 's.41/s.59 as amended by No.9': 62,000 a month, leave 11 January–10 May 2026 in five per-window entries. The March slip has nine unpaid days (12–20 March) at 2,000, so gross 44,000, SSO 875/875 and PIT 1,104.16. The test also checks that no version, scheme or adhoc row cites the 2019 copy.

### TH-S3 Gazette originals — 2026-09-28

- **Re-verified.** curl (browser user agent and referer), WebFetch and the Browser pane all get the Gazette's "Access Denied … blocked by our security systems" page for 100888, 98728, 89818, 84794 and 86102 (the Chrome extension was not connected). www.mol.go.th returns an Incapsula block. The issue stands.
- **EWF, read from the Gazette's own PDFs.** The Internet Archive holds captures of two of them: [84794](https://web.archive.org/web/20260913004819/https://ratchakitcha.soc.go.th/documents/84794.pdf) (captured 13 September 2026, SHA-256 `90bb10e0…13e715f4`) and [86102](https://web.archive.org/web/20250919123501/https://ratchakitcha.soc.go.th/documents/86102.pdf) (captured 19 September 2025, SHA-256 `b2664e07…ca73d1d2`). Their text layers are garbled, so the pages were rendered to images and read. The commencement decree (vol.142 part 59 Kor pp.4–5, 14 September 2025): s.3 repeals the B.E.2567 decree; s.4 starts collection of EWF savings and contributions on 1 October 2026. The rate regulation (vol.142 part 60 Kor pp.3–4, 15 September 2025, signed 9 September 2025): cl.1 in force 1 October 2026; cl.3(1) 0.25% employee savings and 0.25% employer contribution, 1 October 2026 to 30 September 2031; cl.3(2) 0.5% each from 1 October 2031. This matches the Council of State copy and the seed, so no figure moves.
- **s.33 base, read from page images.** The SSO copy ([47755](https://www.sso.go.th/wpr/download/download_by_pool_file/47755), SHA-256 `529c7de6…d3f6b07b`) is the Gazette's own two-page PDF, vol.142 part 81 Kor pp.5–6. Only its text layer is broken, and the rendered page reads cleanly. Signed 11 December 2025. cl.1 in force 1 January 2026; cl.2 repeals MR No.7 (B.E.2538). cl.3 gives the base at not less than THB1,650 a month and not more than: (1) THB17,500, 1 January 2026 to 31 December 2028; (2) THB20,000, 1 January 2029 to 31 December 2031; (3) THB23,000 from 1 January 2032. This matches the seed, so no figure moves. 98728 has no archive capture; 47755 is the same instrument.
- **LPA No.9 (89818).** The only capture is the 403 page. The figures rest on the Council of State consolidation (TH-S2), not on 89818.
- **Flood relief (100888, TH-SS-02).** The only capture is a 301 redirect. The notice is not in the SSO's ประกาศกระทรวงแรงงาน list. It stays **OPEN** on the register's transcription, corroborated by the PRD and press reports. The Ministry of Labour's English release (cabinet approval, not the notice) is Incapsula-blocked.
- **Seed (every version, in place).** The source instructions now say how each instrument was read. The four versions from 1 October 2026 also cite both archive captures.
- **Golden:** 'Gazette originals: EWF and s.33 base at each cl.3 boundary (TH-S3)' prices 30,000 a month. September 2026: SSO 875 each side, no EWF. October 2026: 875 and 75. December 2028: 875 and 75. January 2029: 1,000 and 75. September 2031: 1,000 and 75. October 2031: 1,000 and 150. January 2032: 1,150 and 150. The test also checks that every EWF-bearing version cites both captures.

### TH-J1 training-leave seed — 2026-09-29

[Labour Protection Act s.36 and s.52](https://searchlaw.ocs.go.th/council-of-state/#/public/doc/ZGN3NXk0eENvNjBSdjRnT2NsdjFTQT09) and the Ministry-hosted [Ministerial Regulation No.5 B.E.2541, cls.1–3](https://www.mol.go.th/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2019/03/77-2b-2541-a005-5.pdf) support separate leave codes on every existing sealed TH version. `TRAINING_LEAVE` defaults to unpaid by the Act's statutory minimum (ss.56–59 contain no pay duty for s.36), while `leave.agreed_fraction` preserves a more favourable approved pay agreement; the regulation requires eligible purpose, at least seven days' notice and available evidence, and permits employer refusal after at least 30 days or three occasions in the year or on demonstrated business harm. `YOUNG_WORKER_TRAINING_LEAVE` preserves normal working-day pay for a worker under 18 for the first 30 such days in a year under s.52; later days use any separately agreed paid share. Age is checked in each charged-day pay expression; the catalogue leaves eligibility open so an ineligible approved entry cannot evade the salary deduction. These are seeded catalogue/pricing branches, **not** completed entitlement adjudication.

Open: leave approval does not record statutory training purpose, organiser approval, notice delivery, prior occasions or proven business harm. The catalogue checks age on the leave-year rule date for eligibility and on each charged day for pay; birthday-boundary access and s.52's calendar-day wording need a shared planner/selector review. The leave planner skips rest/holiday days, so it cannot yet prove whether a s.52 leave event's first 30 statutory days were counted correctly. No test/check/build or production probe was run in this jurisdiction pass.

[Labour Protection Act ss.23–26](https://www.mol.go.th/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2018/03/301.pdf), [Ministerial Regulation No.3 B.E.2541](https://www.mol.go.th/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2018/07/168.pdf) and the [Ministry's 36-hour summary](https://www.mol.go.th/employee/%E0%B8%AA%E0%B8%B4%E0%B8%97%E0%B8%98%E0%B8%B4%E0%B8%AB%E0%B8%99%E0%B9%89%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%97%E0%B8%B5%E0%B9%88%E0%B8%99%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%A2%E0%B8%88%E0%B9%89%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%87-%E0%B8%A5%E0%B8%B9%E0%B8%81%E0%B8%88%E0%B9%89%E0%B8%B2%E0%B8%87) supply the 48/42 normal-week limits, agreed redistribution to at most nine normal hours in a day, prior consent for each overtime/holiday-work occasion, and 36 combined overtime/holiday-work hours in a week. All eight TH versions seed the normal-week and combined-work ceilings. Payroll totals complete Monday–Sunday weeks and refuses normal-week or 36-hour breaches; recorded premium work must have an approved paid quantity and a consent timestamp before its first punch (only a recorded Thai s.24/s.25 exception with a reference can bypass consent), and TH incentive hours refuse. A nine-hour normal day needs a prior dated agreement and offsetting shorter normal day in the same week; without the agreement its ninth worked hour is priced as overtime. Daily/hourly/piece-rate redistributed days above eight refuse until the statutory 1.5× supplement has a separate pay route. Focused golden tests cover the nine-hour agreement, shorter-day offset, prior consent, and 36-hour boundary. The pattern write gate also refuses a seven-WORK-day cycle, and payroll refuses an inherited seven-WORK-day plan. HR’s work-day record sheet captures both timestamps; the Thai scheduling workbook issues a long-form Overtime sheet with both fields, including a zero-overtime redistribution row. Open: the asserted Thai s.24/s.25 exception needs external evidence review, and the existing owner rule excludes clock time before shift start from worked hours. Section 27 now reads saved timed work gaps for punched work and `break_start_time` for scheduled work without punches. Split subhour rests require a prior saved agreement, and at least two hours of immediate overtime require a preceding twenty-minute gap. Rest above two hours and continuous-work/emergency exceptions refuse until their wage and eligibility treatment is implemented.

[Labour Protection Act ss.56 and 62](https://searchlaw.ocs.go.th/council-of-state/#/public/doc/ZGN3NXk0eENvNjBSdjRnT2NsdjFTQT09) give weekly-holiday wages to every worker except those paid daily, hourly or by piece; a worker already entitled to holiday wages earns at least 1× extra for holiday work, while one not entitled earns at least 2×. All eight TH premium bands now include `WEEKLY` in the paid-holiday branch and route `PIECE_RATE` to the unpaid-holiday branch. Per-unit piece-work amounts and the actual paid holiday base remain shared product work.

### Round 6 — 2026-09-28

- **Closed or narrowed:** TH-WAGE-01/-02 transfer between worksites (golden, dated terms row); TH-WORK-05 seven-hour normal day (golden); TH-LEAVE-06 s.32 certificate threshold, which was a wrong transcription fixed in place in all eight versions (`evidence_after_days` 3 → 2) (golden).
- **Round 6 engine corrections:** work days can record a separate site; dated terms mark s.23 hazardous work; piece wages and the last-N-workday severance path are covered by goldens. Piece severance now loads a bounded historical day window and refuses if a recent scheduled workday lacks piece earnings or explicit absence; work histories sparser than one day per week beyond 400 weeks refuse rather than extrapolate.
- **Unreachable source:** the ratchakitcha flood-relief PDF (TH-SS-02, TH-S3 remainder) returned 403 / Access Denied again, and the Wayback Machine answered 429 or was refused.

### TH-EM-01 s.33 lower age bound — 2026-09-29

- **Engine fixed in every SSO version (transcription, in place).** The `assessed_on` test read `age_on(employment.service_start) < 15`, so a worker hired under 15 was never insured. s.33 paras 1–2 and s.38 (re-read 29 Sep 2026 in the Council of State consolidation as the [Ministry's updated Act](https://www.mol.go.th/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2021/02/1-%E0%B8%9E%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%9A.%E0%B8%9B%E0%B8%A3%E0%B8%B0%E0%B8%81%E0%B8%B1%E0%B8%99%E0%B8%AA%E0%B8%B1%E0%B8%87%E0%B8%84%E0%B8%A1-update.pdf) serves it) make an employee "ไม่ต่ำกว่าสิบห้าปีบริบูรณ์และไม่เกินหกสิบปีบริบูรณ์" an insured person: a status, not an entry test. s.38 ends it only on death or leaving employment. The lower bound now reads `age_on(period.end) < 15`; the upper bound stays at entry (para.2 keeps an insured person past 60).
- **Default (law silent, owner rule 2026-09-28).** The Act does not apportion the month in which a worker turns 15. The bound is read on the period end, the day the monthly wage is paid; s.47 para.1 deducts from wages paid to an insured person, so the whole month's wage is contributory in the birthday month.
- **Golden:** 's.33 para.1 is a status test' — February 2026, 30,000, born 1996-06-01, hired 2010-07-01 (14 at hire): SSO 875/875, PIT 164.58 (360,000 − 100,000 − 60,000 − 10,500 = 189,500 → 1,975 ÷ 12). The same holds for the fixture's default age 40 with a 2001-01-01 hire.
